- Measured pass (M9-05): the alloc probe now runs to completion (exit through the harness's value check). `v.iter()` on a `Vec<u32>` passed `&v` straight to `<[T]>::iter`: tyck found the method through the overloaded `Deref` (`Vec<T>` -> `[T]`) but lowering never emitted the deref, so the slice reference carried an extra indirection and `*x` read pointer bits (the String's `len()` through `String` -> `str` had the same shape). tyck records the `Deref::Target` a method-call receiver was dereferenced through (`receiver_derefs`, per expression); lowering borrows the receiver (loading `&&T` down to one layer) and emits a new `DEREF_CALL` rvalue (`Deref::deref`, or `DerefMut::deref_mut` for a `&mut self` callee) resolved per receiver type at emission like `ITER_NEXT`. deref-method fixture (`w.bump()` through `DerefMut`, `w.total()` / `r.total()` through `Deref`) — 56/56 in both gcc and tcc lanes. Gates: gcc test, clang strict, tcc, ASan/UBSan all green (echoed exit lines verified; commit gated).
- Measured pass (M9-05): with the deref in place the Vec side of the alloc probe is exact (sums 0/3/9/18) and the String stayed empty: `extend_from_slice`'s `self.spec_extend(other.iter())` took the first candidate whose parameters accept the arguments — `impl<T, I: Iterator<Item = T>> SpecExtend<T, I>` (its `iterator: I` accepts anything; bounds are not modelled) instead of `impl SpecExtend<&T, slice::Iter<T>>` (`append_elements`), so the generic `extend_desugared` iterated `&u8` items into a `Vec<u8>`. Method lookup now treats a compatible candidate with a bare-generic parameter facing a concrete argument as loose: it keeps scanning the same autoderef step's variants for a candidate written for the argument's shape and only stands if none exists (looked up afresh when promoted). spec-extend fixture (`impl<I> Ext<u32, I>` vs `impl Ext<u32, Cursor>`) — 57/57 in both gcc and tcc lanes. Gates: gcc test, clang strict, tcc, ASan/UBSan all green (echoed exit lines verified; commit gated).

- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): the string probe split into six roots (`format!`, `Vec<String>` + `push_str`/`push`, `to_string` + `+`, `String + &str`, `Box::new`/`*boxed`, `as_bytes()[0]`). `p_add` and `p_bytes` are exact; `Box::new` bottomed out in 1.90's `box_new` intrinsic (new shim: a heap cell holding the value, so the box reads like any pointer to a `T` slot); `<T as ConvertVec>::to_vec` reached `impl<T: Clone>`'s partial default before `impl<T: Copy>` (bounds are not modelled) — impl resolution now lets an accepting method without a u-MIR body yield to a later one that has a body. Debug aids: `UMIR opaque …` (lowering), `UBODY unexpanded macro …`, and the lesson that `CM_TYCK_DEBUG` prints 40 lines unless `CM_TYCK_DEBUG_LIMIT` is raised. Remaining stubs on this rung: `Vec::set_len` and `ConvertVec::to_vec` (alloc, tyck-partial), `as_mut_ptr` / `count` / `align_offset` (core partials); `format!`'s root body is tyck-partial. - Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): dependency value paths. `Vec::set_len` (and `from_parts_in`, `from_raw_parts_in`) went partial on `core::ub_checks::check_library_ub` — `assert_unsafe_precondition!`'s expansion — and the probe's `format!` on `alloc::__export::must_use` / `alloc::fmt::format` / `core::fmt::rt::Argument::new_display`: the import walk looked the first segment up in the resolver's own string table before considering dependencies, so a crate that never writes `use core::…` (or writes `core::` only inside a body) could not reach `core` at all, and a builtin invoked through another crate's re-export spelled its machinery with that crate's prefix. The walk now delegates an uninterned first segment to a registered dependency by its raw bytes, and builtins always name the registered `core` dependency. paths probe: `core::ub_checks::check_library_ub()`, `core::hint::must_use(x)`, `core::fmt::rt::Argument::new_display(&x)` all emit through `--with-core`. - Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): two more resolution gaps. Inside alloc, `core::ub_checks::check_library_ub` reached the first segment through the injected dependency prelude (`core` itself), and the walk then treated that binding's target as a module of alloc's own graph — a prelude binding carrying a dependency tag now delegates the rest of the path to that crate's resolver. In core, `align_offset`'s nested `const unsafe fn mod_inv` was parsed as a `const` path expression: `const` followed by `unsafe`/`async`/`extern` now starts a nested item. nested-const-fn fixture — 58/58 in gcc and tcc lanes; gates pending.
- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): `MaybeUninit::as_mut_ptr` (`self as *mut _ as *mut T`) went partial because a cast target whose pointee is left to inference never met the reference being cast; tyck now unifies an inferred pointee with the source pointer's pointee. cast-infer fixture — 59/59 in gcc and tcc lanes; gates pending (folded into the next gated batch).
- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): inside alloc the expansion of `assert_unsafe_precondition!` spells `::core::ub_checks::check_library_ub` — an *absolute* path — and both first-segment dependency delegations required a relative path, so the walk searched alloc's own root for `core` (core itself only survives through `extern crate self as core`). `::core::x` now delegates to the registered dependency like `core::x`. 59/59 fixtures in gcc and tcc lanes; split-probe chain pending.
- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): `Vec::set_len` types (`vec_string(3)` = 3, `set_len`/`as_mut_ptr` stubs gone). `to_string` then overflowed in `to_vec`: `_fmt(..).to_string()` on a `&str` receiver picked `impl ToString for T` with `T = &str` and passed the `&str` itself where that impl's `&self` is `&&str` (the generated `impl SpecToString for &str` derefs once), so a fat pointer lost a layer. Two changes: tyck records the autoderef step at which a method was found (`receiver_steps`) and lowering derives the receiver adjustment from it (autoref at step 0 even for a reference receiver; `&self` at step *n* passes the reference reached after *n−1* loads; by-value `self` loads *n* times); and the probe prefers rustc's by-value order — a `&self` method of the pointee (self type = the receiver) beats a `&self` method of the reference type needing autoref (`out.write_byte(b)` on `&mut dyn Write` is the object's method; `s.to_string()` on `&str` is `<str as ToString>`). autoref-step fixture (`impl Show for &Item` on `&Item`/`&&Item`, by-value twin) — 60/60 in gcc and tcc lanes; gates pending.
- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): the emitter side of the autoderef step. With lowering shaping the receiver operand from the recorded step, emission must read it the same way: an autoref at step 0 is a local typed as its referent (already Self), a reference reached at a later step is `&Self`. The impl-callee fit check, the declaration-path peel, the direct-impl generic binding and the signature binder (`&self`'s written type is `Self`; its kind carries the `&`) now peel exactly one layer only at steps ≥ 1, so `impl<T: Disp> ToS for T` on a `&Item` receiver binds `T = Item`, not `&Item`. The probe's by-value preference looks at every impl kind of the pointee (a specific `impl Spec for &Item` must not hide the blanket impl for `Item`). byvalue-first fixture (rustc: 11) — 61/61 in gcc and tcc lanes; gates pending.
- Measured pass (M9-05, alloc-string rung): `to_string` exact; `format!` returned a dangling String because `Arguments::as_str`'s `match (self.pieces, self.args) { ([], []) => .., ([s], []) => .., _ => None }` took the first arm for any input — slice patterns had no lowering at all (they acted as wildcards, binding nothing) — and the `Some` arm's `f(t)` called a fn *item* held in a local (`ToOwned::to_owned` as `F: FnOnce`) which emission rendered as an unsupported call. Slice patterns now lower: a new `SLICE_LEN` rvalue (the pair's length through references, or an array type's length) feeds a length range test (`== n`, or `>= n` with a rest), then each fixed element before the rest is reached by index (its address behind a reference, matching ergonomics; the value otherwise) and bound or tested; `rest @ ..` and elements after a rest are still unbound. A local of fn-item type used as a callee dispatches directly to that item's instance. slice-pat-tuple (3371) and fn-item-value fixtures — 63/63 in gcc and tcc lanes; gates pending.
- **Milestone (M9-05, alloc-string rung): the six split roots are exact through real core + alloc** — `format!("{}-{:x}", i, i * 16)` (length 4), `Vec<String>` + `push_str`/`push`, `u32::to_string() + "!"`, `String + &str`, `Box::new`/`*boxed`, `as_bytes()[0]`. Three-crate census: tyck 25,195 typed / 570 partial / 198 error nodes; remaining stubs are core partials (`mod_inv`, `fold`, `count`, `compiletime`) and trait declarations without bodies (`next`, `clone`, `addr`) not reached at run time. Next rung: std hello world (`println!`, `String::from`, `vec!`) through the chain core → alloc → cfg-if → libc → std with a host shim harness; `PROBE_MAX_DEPS` raised to 16.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung opened): atomic intrinsic shims — `atomic_load`/`atomic_store`/`atomic_xchg`/`atomic_xadd`/`atomic_xsub`/`atomic_and`/`atomic_or`/`atomic_xor`/`atomic_nand`/`atomic_max`/`atomic_min`/`atomic_umax`/`atomic_umin` as plain loads and read-modify-writes at `T`'s width, `atomic_cxchg`/`atomic_cxchgweak` returning a `(old, swapped)` pair block, fences as no-ops (single-threaded C99; the `const ORD` generics are ignored). `OnceLock`/`ReentrantLock` behind std's `stdout()` need them. Fixtures 63/63 in gcc and tcc lanes. The std chain is `core → alloc → cfg-if → libc → std` (vendored `cfg-if-1.0.1`, `libc-0.2.174`; later `unwind`, `panic_abort`, `panic_unwind`, `hashbrown-0.15.4`, `std_detect`, `rustc-demangle-0.1.25`); note a chain must pass `--emit-umir-c` (or `--body-census`) or alloc's type-position macros (`impl SpecToString for to_string_str_wrap_in_ref!(..)`) are left unexpanded and its HIR fails.
- Measured pass (M9-06): `Ordering::SeqCst` — a dependency enum's unit variant spelled `Type::Variant` through an import (or a type alias) — resolved as a type-relative path whose tail tyck matched to the variant, but lowering only saw the TYPE_ASSOC path and emitted an item-path placeholder (`AtomicU32::store`'s `match order` then dereferenced 0). tyck records the variant in `method_targets` at both fake-variant sites (enum base, alias base) and lowering treats such a path as a VARIANT value. alias-variant fixture — 64/64 in gcc and tcc lanes. The probe tool prints graph-error details (`detail=<kind>: <message>`), needed for libc's `item macro planning failed`. Gates pending.
- Measured pass (M9-06): statics have storage. A `static` rendered as a function re-running its initializer, so `static COUNTER: AtomicU32` (a transparent wrapper around its scalar) was rebuilt at every use and `&COUNTER` addressed a temporary copy — every atomic read saw the initial value. A static now renders as a once-initialized cached slot (`<sym>_addr()` returns its address, `<sym>()` its value); `&STATIC` and a static receiver's autoref lower to a `STATIC_ADDR` rvalue naming that slot (`static mut` writes through the same address). Core-only atomic probe: `store 7; fetch_add 3; load; compare_exchange(10, 100); load` → 100101007, exact.
- Measured pass (M9-06, libc rung): libc's crate root is a `cfg_if!` cascade whose bodies are items. `$it:item` fragments at the end of a pattern matched one token each (the opaque-fragment search grows from one node and `item` accepted any node), so `$(#[$m] $it)*` re-emitted `#[cfg] mod #[cfg] x #[cfg] ;` — an `item` fragment now spans attribute prefixes plus everything through its first top-level `;` or brace group (a `;` right after the group included). Generated `use` items are accepted by the graph and HIR (with generated `static`, extern blocks and `macro_rules!`); `CM_MACRO_DEBUG=1` dumps a failed reparse's transcribed text. cfg-if-items fixture (`prelude!()` module + generated `pub use`, 108) — 65/65 in gcc and tcc lanes; the libc-shaped scratch crate builds and runs. - Measured pass (M9-06, libc rung): generated out-of-line modules (`cfg_if! { mod unix; }`) — the graph builds them through the same file loader as source `mod x;` declarations and HIR lowers generated modules whether inline or not; the libc-shaped scratch crate with `mod primitives;` / `mod other;` files builds and runs (108). - Committed as `6530322a` (gates: gcc/strict/tcc/ASan lanes, whole-core census 22,911 typed, core probes 3/3, alloc probes 2/2). libc as a root crate now loads 367 sources and 3 modules before one graph error (the error record is stored but the probe's print loop shows nothing — under investigation). - Measured pass (M9-06, libc rung): libc's single graph error was a parser gap — `pub type SceKernelVTimerHandler = unsafe extern "C" fn(uid: SceUid, ..)` (`extern "ABI"` fn-pointer types with named parameters; `unix/mod.rs:602` on Linux) — plus C-variadic foreign declarations (`fn printf(fmt: *const c_char, ...)`): the type grammar takes `[for<..>] [unsafe] [extern ["abi"]] fn(name: T, .., ...)`, function items carry `is_variadic` through the AST into the HIR signature, and HIR's extern-block preflight no longer demands `unsafe extern` (edition-2021 `extern "C" { .. }`). The first cut of body-local `type X = ..;` detection broke core (`slice/memchr.rs:122` `type Chunk = usize;` went to the block-item parser, which rejected type aliases; every downstream gate went red until the accept list took `TYPE_ALIAS`). The variadic foreign shim only forwarded the declared parameters (`cm_u_1_2(p0) { host_sum(p0); }` — gcc/tcc passed by register luck, clang returned garbage): a variadic extern is now called by its host symbol directly at the call site. Body-local `unsafe impl`/`unsafe trait`/`unsafe extern "C" fn` (hashbrown `raw/mod.rs:4316`) and loop labels (`'outer: for ..`; label dropped, so a labelled `break` targets the innermost loop) parse. libc then failed in `primitives.rs` three ways: (1) a macro-generated `mod primitives;` inherited no textual macro scope (`cm_propagate_inherited_macro_histories` compared the parent's history against the generated item's synthetic reparse span; it now anchors a generated `mod` at its source invocation) — "no declaration-ordered macro binding"; (2) `#[cfg(all(.., // FIXME .. \n ..))]` keeps its line comments in the predicate text and the text-based cfg parser choked — "generated-item cfg or cfg_attr expansion failed" (also hashbrown `control/group/mod.rs`); `cm_cfg_skip_space` skips `//` and `/* */`; (3) an else-less `cfg_if! { if #[cfg(..)] { 6 items } }` is tried against the `.. } else ..` rule first and the `$($it:item)*` search backtracked through every partition of the items (an item candidate's maximum was "everything remaining") — "macro_rules backtracking step limit exceeded"; an item fragment's maximum is now its extent. Standalone `macros.rs + primitives.rs` crate: 21 items. `CM_MACRO_DEBUG` prints the cfg diagnostic at the planner's cfg failure. Fixtures fn-ptr-abi (an `extern "C" fn(x: u32) -> u32` alias called through a value; `host_sum(3, x, 10, 100)` through a variadic extern), local-type, local-unsafe-impl — 68/68 in gcc, tcc and clang lanes; cfg unit tests take comments; gates pending (libc/hashbrown reruns in flight).
- Committed as `daf55d9b` (gates: gcc/strict/tcc/ASan lanes, whole-core census 22,911 typed / 22,500 lowered, core probes 3/3, alloc probes 2/2, fixtures 68/68). Measured pass (M9-06, libc rung, continued): with `primitives.rs` clean, libc's `unix/mod.rs` failed on 18 `__item!` invocations — generated by `s!` expansions inside the (itself generated) `unix` module. Standalone copies narrowed it to the crate root's `prelude!()`: a root with a *generated* pending invocation defers building non-`macro_use` modules, but the staged replan still walked every unit's generated pendings, so unix's `__item!` ran with no owner module (`module=0`) and errored instead of waiting; units whose module is still deferred now skip the round. Two more libc shapes: `s!` emits `#[::core::prelude::v1::derive(..)]` in token-spaced generated text (`:: core :: prelude :: v1 :: derive (..)`) — the cfg expander's attribute head now takes a path, and the HIR derive/attribute matchers compare the final path segment (space-tolerant); and hashbrown's `use crate::alloc::alloc::{Layout, handle_alloc_error}` (a `crate::`-qualified path through the root's `extern crate alloc;`) delegates to the dependency when the root has no module of that name (scratch `use crate::core::cmp::max` typed and rendered). Full libc source copy (no deps): graph errors 0, 13 modules. libc over core then: 9 unresolved imports from `prelude!`'s `pub(crate) use ::core::clone::Clone;` (a leading `::` names an extern crate in editions 2018+; absolute leaves whose first segment is a registered dependency now delegate) — imports 0 — then HIR at `unix/mod.rs:392` `extern "C" { pub static in6addr_loopback: in6_addr; }`: foreign statics are accepted (preflight, reservation, HIR validity), lowered bodiless with `value_item.is_foreign`, and rendered as the host symbol's address (`<sym>_addr() { extern char NAME[]; .. }`); fixture foreign-static (value read and `&HOST_LEVEL` through a raw pointer, host mutates between calls) — 69/69. hashbrown over core+alloc: `Layout` imported through `crate::alloc::alloc` is alloc's re-export of `core::alloc::Layout`; the delegated binding carried alloc's dependency numbering (`dep=2`) for a core declaration, so HIR lookup failed ("local-crate type-path resolution failed"); a delegated binding's inner tag is now mapped to the outer resolver's entry for the same crate (scratch `Layout::new::<u32>().size()` over core+alloc typed and rendered). libc then walked further into HIR: generated `extern "C"` blocks and their children (cfg_if! branches generate whole blocks) are retained; `#[link(name = .., kind = "static", ..)]` and lint/doc/cfg attributes on extern blocks are accepted (linking is the host's concern); `pub fn statx(..)` beside `pub struct statx { .. }` no longer collides (namespace masks are item-aware: a named-field struct, union or enum names only a type). hashbrown: `[Tag; Group::WIDTH]` takes a `Type::CONST` array length (unique struct/enum/union by name, same module first; `WIDTH = size_of::<Self>()` stays CONST_UNEVALUATED); `&mut dyn FnMut(usize) -> bool` puts `Output` on the supertrait `FnOnce` — lowering and the HIR type validator accept equalities declared by a trait reachable through supertraits, and `dyn P` vtables now lay out P's supertrait closure (supertraits first, then P) in both the table and the slot lookup (a `dyn Sub<Out = u32>` calling a `Base` method dispatched to slot 0 = `twice`; fixture dyn-super-assoc 32 exact). Fixtures 71/71. libc's `use crate::prelude::*` glob re-imported core's `Clone` on its *macro* side (the builtin derive) with no HIR macro definition — HIR rejected the module's imports; targetless macro-namespace bindings are compacted away (the trait binding stays). Then `unix/mod.rs:46` `s!`: `timeval` carries `#[cfg(not(gnu_time_bits64))] tv_usec: suseconds_t` beside `#[cfg(gnu_time_bits64)] tv_usec: __suseconds64_t` — the parser discarded field attributes, so both fields survived and the second's type was unresolved; fields keep their attributes and the module graph compacts cfg-inactive struct/union/variant fields in place (fixture cfg-field: twin fields + an enum-variant field, 42 exact). `CM_LOWER_DEBUG` names unresolved type paths, rejected module-import bindings and unresolved array lengths. Then `linux/mod.rs`: `c_enum!` writes `pub type pid_type = c_enum!(@repr);` — the body expander now expands type-position macros in item types too (type aliases, const/static types, struct/union/variant fields, fn signatures); array lengths take `FD_SETSIZE as usize / ULONG_SIZE` (casts), `crate::MAX_ADDR_LEN` (`::`-paths through the import resolver), and `0x051C`/`0o`/`0b`/`16usize` literals (fixtures cast-const-len, crate-const-len, radix-len; 74/74). Whole-core after the field compaction: typed 22,909 (was 22,911) — the two are RefCell bodies whose `#[cfg(feature = "debug_refcell")]` fields are now (correctly) dropped; the statements that use them are cfg-gated the same way and should drop too — the RefCell bodies show no tyck errors, so the two are elsewhere (open). **libc over core: HIR errors 0, 46,402 items, 28,893 bodies / 28,429 typed (libc contributes 5,520 of its 5,522), 28,018 lowered, exit 0 — the libc rung is green.** Unit-test contracts updated: `dyn Iterator<Item = ()>` with `Item` on a supertrait is accepted; `#[link]`ed extern blocks are accepted; `N_POWERS_OF_FIVE = 1usize` folds (suffixed literal); `#[diagnostic::on_unimplemented]` must keep its tool namespace — attribute prefix stripping is limited to `core::prelude::v1::`/`std::prelude::v1::`. Next rung (unwind, std_detect): `cfg_if::cfg_if!` — a qualified invocation into a registered dependency resolves through that crate's macro artifact (`cm_dependency_macro_artifact_lookup`), and `use cfg_if::cfg_if; cfg_if! {..}` no longer reads its own delegated import binding as a competing local macro (the leaf also no longer counts as its own import cycle). A qualified invocation is retained as its own consumer import; a generated out-of-line `mod libunwind;` keeps its source-written inner attributes; `unsafe extern "C-unwind" { .. }` (and `system`) blocks are retained. **unwind over core+cfg_if+libc: HIR errors 0, 28,902 bodies / 28,438 typed / 28,027 lowered, exit 0.** std_detect: nested `macro_rules!` inside `features!`'s transcriber writes the inner macro's `$t`; an unbound metavariable in a transcriber now passes through as `$` `t` tokens. Core gate: core-fmt2-probe regressed to `Some(0x0)` — a `dyn Debug` dispatch inside a closure capture whose receiver's recorded principal trait does not reach `Debug`, so the closure-ordered slot lookup returned -1 and the call fell to a non-dyn path; the lookup now falls back to the method's own trait (a `dyn P` table and a `dyn T` index agree when T's closure is P's prefix — the previous behaviour for every table); core-fmt2 exact again. std_detect: `pub use x86::*;` now carries the generated `pub(crate) enum Feature` (crate-visible items pass a public glob at the narrower visibility); panic_unwind: `use unwind as uw;` binds the bare aliased dependency crate. **std_detect over core+cfg_if+libc: HIR errors 0, 29,003 bodies / 28,539 typed / 28,125 lowered, exit 0.** The two whole-core bodies lost to field compaction are the *derived* `Debug::fmt` of `BorrowError`/`BorrowMutError`: derive expansion ran on the freshly parsed unit before any cfg evaluation, so it still derived the `#[cfg(feature = "debug_refcell")] location` field; cfg-inactive fields are now compacted before `cm_derive_expand` (`CM_TYCK_DEBUG_PARTIAL=1` prints each partial body's owner, the diff that named them). **panic_unwind over core+alloc+cfg_if+libc+unwind: HIR errors 0, 31,297 bodies / 30,722 typed / 30,122 lowered, exit 0** — every std dependency (core, alloc, cfg_if, libc, unwind, panic_abort, panic_unwind, hashbrown, std_detect, rustc_demangle) lowers end to end. The std hello chain (all ten plus std) builds every dependency with HIR errors 0; std itself opened with `#[allow(unused_imports)] #[macro_use] extern crate alloc as alloc_crate;` — `macro_use` on an extern crate now records the crate's extern name and unqualified invocations fall back to `[crate, name]` lookups through the dependency macro artifacts (scratch `#[macro_use] extern crate cfg_if as cfg_if_crate; cfg_if! {..}` builds). Crate-visible items pass a *glob* re-export only (a single `pub use X as Y` keeps its contract of not carrying the crate-visible constructor). std as root over its ten dependencies then failed reparsing `include!("keyword_docs.rs")`: the name now resolved through the macro_use fallback to core's `#[rustc_builtin_macro] macro_rules! include { .. => {{ }} }` and transcribed `{}`; a dependency-resolved builtin `include` (attribute text keeps its `#[..]` wrapper) is routed to the authenticated include splice like a local one, and `core`/`std` are implicit macro_use crates (rustc injects `#[macro_use] extern crate core;` for no_std crates) — scratch `extern crate core; include!("inc.rs")` over core splices and types. std then loaded 634 sources / 247 modules with six graph errors: `impl (FnOnce() -> Capture) + Send` (a parenthesized trait bound in an impl/dyn bound list), `macro spec_str($T:ty) { .. }` in statement position (std's OsString), and `#[path = "../../backtrace/src/lib.rs"] mod backtrace_rs;` whose nested `mod backtrace;` must resolve beside that file (a `#[path]` file module owns its directory, like mod.rs) — fixture paren-bound-macro (a parenthesized bound, a `dyn` call, a body-local `macro bump($x:expr)`; 76/76). Deferred: an `impl Trait`-returning value unsized to `&dyn` is unsized with its opaque type (no vtable → segfault); the scratch that showed it is kept out of the fixtures. Next std error (638 sources / 254 modules): `use crate::sys::thread_local::local_pointer; local_pointer! {..}` — an unqualified invocation of a `use`-imported macro (`pub(crate) macro local_pointer {..}` re-exported twice) never consulted the import resolver; it now resolves single-segment names in the macro namespace (imports initialized on demand) — fixture macro-reexport (77/77). In std the same name still failed: the re-export is generated by `cfg_if!` in a module the *next* round builds, and the replan errored at once. A missing macro binding is now deferred while modules are still deferred or other units still have pending invocations; a round errors only when it makes no progress, where progress means a unit's pending set shrank or new units appeared (a unit is re-planned from scratch each round, so re-resolving the same invocations is not progress — the first cut of this looped to the staging limit and broke core's graph, caught by the staged-macro-use fixture and gate 72). Next std error: `static_assert!(@usize_eq: size_of::<NonNull<()>>(), 8)` → `const _: [(); size_of::<NonNull<()>>()] = [(); 8]` — the array-length text capture counted `<`/`>` but a `>>` token closes two, masking the `]`; and the const-length evaluator folds `size_of::<T>()`/`align_of::<T>()` for primitives, pointer-shaped types (`&T`, `*T`, fn, `NonNull`/`Box`/`Rc`/`Arc`, their `Option`s), unit and arrays, plus `&`, `|`, `>>`, `%` (pointer sizes follow `pointer_bits`; `[u8; 7 % 4]`, `[u8; 16 / size_of::<u8>()]`, 16-bit `16 / size_of::<*const ()>()` = 8 and `impl i8 { [u8; size_of::<i8>()] }` moved from the rejected to the folded contracts). Next std error: `cfg_if::cfg_if! { .. }` *inside* `unsafe extern "C" { .. }` (sys/cmath.rs) — item macros expand inside extern-block containers like in a module (children attach to the block; a non-module container resolves names in the unit's file module) — expansions reparse in a new extern-block fragment context so edition-2024 `pub safe fn ..;` is permitted — fixture extern-block-macro (`unsafe extern "C" { declare_host!(host_add); }` with a `safe fn`; 79/79). Then `sys/env_consts.rs`: `macro cfg_unordered(..) { .. }` invoked with braces — the paren-parameter declarative form accepted only paren-delimited invocations; any delimiter now (fixture decl-macro-brace, 80/80). std re-probe in flight. hashbrown over core+alloc: HIR errors 0 (44,813 items), tyck 26,331 bodies / 25,724 typed (core+alloc alone: 25,191), u-MIR 25,081 lowered / 643 blocked — the crate lowers end to end; whether its byte-level group tricks run on the slot model is a later question (std's HashMap is not on the hello-world path). Graph errors now name the missing macro; `CM_MACRO_DEBUG` dumps the macro-scope history at a missing binding and the rejected attribute head.
- Committed as `e5441bca` (gates: gcc/strict/tcc/ASan lanes 86, whole-core census 22,912 typed / 22,501 lowered, core probes 3/3, alloc probes 2/2, fixtures 80/80). std root 14 (all ten dependencies lower with HIR errors 0) stopped in `sys/random/mod.rs`: `cfg_if::cfg_if!` over 19 `else if` arms failed with "macro_rules backtracking step limit exceeded"; raising the default budget from 100,000 to 2,000,000 steps did not help.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): the cascade was exponential, not long. cfg-if's `@__items` recursion carries a growing `( $( $no:meta , )* )` list and its terminal rule (`@__items ( .. ) ;` then end) only fails *after* that group is consumed, so every `meta` capture was retried at every extent (`target_os` alone, then `target_os = "linux"`): 2^d partitions at depth d — N=16 arms passed, N=20 blew 2,000,000 steps. A `meta` is `SimplePath ( '=' expr | DelimTokenTree )?`, so `cm_rules_meta_extent` now computes its one extent (path segments, then a delimited group or `=` up to the next top-level `,`/`;`) and pins minimum = maximum, like `item`. Fixture `meta-cascade` (cfg-if's macro verbatim, 20 arms, the linux arm 12th) plans instantly; the default budget stays at 2,000,000 (body expansion's value). Core probe unchanged (22,912 typed, 132 error nodes).
- std root 16 (after the `meta` pin): 673 sources / 294 modules (was 638 / 254) before one parse error — `unsafe fn reset_sigpipe(#[allow(unused_variables)] sigpipe: u8)` in `sys/pal/unix/mod.rs`; outer attributes on fn parameters (lints only) are now parsed and dropped (fixture `param-attr`).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 17 then stopped at `sys/pal/unix/mod.rs:170` — `mod sigpipe { pub const DEFAULT: u8 = 0; .. }` declared *inside* `init`'s body and named `sigpipe::DEFAULT` in a match. Body-local modules: the parser accepts `mod name {` as a block item; HIR reserves the module's fns/consts/statics/types as body-local items of the enclosing fn; the u-body's nested scope tags each with its module name so `m::item` (and only that spelling) resolves to it, and a nested const/static now resolves straight to its body-local HIR definition (previously a fresh inference variable). Fixture `local-mod` (consts in a match, a fn call, both through `sigpipe::`). Fixtures 83/83; core probe unchanged.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 18 stopped at `sys/fs/unix.rs` — `enum STATX_STATE{ Unknown = 0, Present, Unavailable }` declared inside `try_statx`'s body. Body-local enums parse as block items and their variants resolve (`STATX_STATE::Present`) through the reserved body-local HIR variant records. That exposed a real codegen bug: an enum→integer `as` cast rendered the aggregate *pointer* — even for module-level enums (`Ordering as i8` had been silently wrong). Slot 0 holds the variant index; `cm_umir_c_render_enum_cast` now maps index → declared discriminant (a plain slot read when discriminants are sequential, else a conditional chain). Fixture `local-enum` (`Unavailable = 9`).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 19 reached 692 sources / 315 modules, then looped to the staging round limit on std's thread_local plumbing. Four defects, reproduced in a four-file scratch crate mirroring std (`#[macro_use] pub mod thread` → `thread_local!` → `$crate::thread::local_impl::thread_local_inner!` → glob re-export → `pub use native::{thread_local_inner}` in a cfg_if!-generated unit → `pub macro`): (1) a qualified lookup demanded `is_public`, rejecting every `pub(crate) macro`; (2) an unqualified import (`use crate::sys::thread_local::local_pointer;`) errored in round 1 because the sys unit had already expanded its cfg_if! whole (its generated `mod native` is only built after the round) — the deferral now also fires when the round already progressed; (3) generated *qualified* invocations were gated out of the local-crate lookup and, once looked up, were applied as textual-scope seeds (useless for a path) — they are now certified as path-keyed `CmItemMacroResolvedGeneratedPath` entries; (4) every replan starts from scratch with only that round's resolutions, so a unit ping-ponged between "thread_local! resolved, child unresolved" and "child resolved, thread_local! deferred" forever, while `units grew` counted as progress each round — resolutions now accumulate per unit (`sticky_resolved`/`sticky_paths`), and a round without a shrinking pending set is progress only if the multiset of deferred names changed. Fixture `thread-local-plumb` (single file, both thread_local! forms, one inside a cfg_if! arm). Core probe unchanged (22,912 typed).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): the enum→integer cast broke `core-fmt2-probe` (segfault) and both alloc probes — not because the cast was wrong but because it exposed a silent mis-resolution: `fmt_int<T: DisplayInt>` calls `(x % base).to_u8()`; the receiver's type `<T as Rem>::Output` was never normalized (the equality `Rem<Output = Self>` lives on `DisplayInt`'s *supertrait*, and the normalizer only read equalities on predicates naming `Rem` directly), and an unnormalized projection matches any inherent impl in method lookup, so the callee became `core::ascii::Char::to_u8` — harmless while both emitted `(uint8_t)x`. `cm_tyck_supertrait_equality` walks a bound's supertrait closure for the equality (Self := the subject). Fixture `supertrait-output` (a local `Rem<Output = Self>` supertrait plus a decoy inherent `to_u8`). Core probe unchanged (22,912 typed); fmt2/fmt/max 3/3 again.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 23 built its whole module graph (692 sources / 315 modules, 0 graph errors) and stopped at 60 import errors. Three shapes, reproduced in two small dependent crates: (1) `pub use core::{assert, r#try, ..}` — the import-tree lexer split `r#try` at `#` ("invalid import tree"); raw identifiers lex as their name; (2) `extern crate alloc as alloc_crate;` — the alias binding named no dependency; it now carries the `alloc` dependency's tag and root; (3) `use crate::marker::PhantomData` where the root re-exports `core::marker` — the leaf walker took the dependency module id for a local module; `cm_resolve_leaf_dependency` now walks the local prefix and delegates from the first dependency-tagged module binding (the same path serves `alloc_crate::vec`). std root 25: 60 → a new family (`Arc`, `BTreeMap`, `Layout`, `GlobalAlloc`, `sockaddr_un`, …).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): with method resolution honest, both alloc probes segfaulted in `core::ptr::Alignment::as_usize` — `transmute::<usize, Alignment>` produced a bare integer while every other fieldless-enum value is an index block, so the new enum→integer cast dereferenced 8. The transmute shim now builds the block of the variant whose declared discriminant equals the integer (and unwraps back to the discriminant), restricted to fieldless enums so `NonZero::new`'s transmute into `Option<NonZero<T>>` keeps its niche path. Fixture `transmute-enum` (a transparent wrapper over a `#[repr(usize)]` enum with `1 << n` discriminants). (A `~(uint32_t)0` seen for `MAX` there was u32's own body-local `MAX_DEC_N` in `impl_Display!`, not a mis-resolution — probed and cleared.)
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 27 — 49 import errors in two families. (1) `pub(super) use libc as netc;` in `sys/net/connection/socket/unix.rs`, re-exported by `pub use unix::*` and named `use netc as c;` by the parent; likewise `os/unix/net`'s `pub(super)` helpers (`sockaddr_un`, `peer_cred`, the ancillary fns): restricted visibilities (`pub(super)`, `pub(in ..)`) were treated as private and dropped by globs — they now count as crate-visible (lenient). (2) The vendored backtrace-rs (`#[path = "../../backtrace/src/lib.rs"] mod backtrace_rs;`) expects std's `--cfg backtrace_in_libstd`: with it, `mod mystd { pub use crate::*; }` replaces `extern crate std as mystd` and the symbolizer arm is `noop` (no gimli/object/addr2line). The probe grew `--cfg NAME[=VALUE]` (appended to the target's cfg entries); the std chain passes `--cfg backtrace_in_libstd`.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std rung): std root 28 → 0 import errors; the crate enters body expansion and HIR lowering. Cleared in turn: `static mut` statics (graph lowering kept only immutable ones; and an assignment to a static rendered `0 /* todo */` — now STATIC_ADDR + STORE_DEREF), `env!("STD_ENV_ARCH")` (body expansion had no env lookup; a getenv-backed one, the chain exports STD_ENV_ARCH=x86_64), `Report<E = Box<dyn Error>>` (an elided object lifetime in a generic default is 'static), `type LazyResolve = impl FnOnce() -> Capture + ..` (a type-alias impl trait lowers to an inference type, unified where a value is built), and the body-macro misses: `syscall!` (weak.rs) expands to `weak!(..)` which is in scope only at the definition module — unqualified body lookups now also consult the definition modules of the enclosing expansions (mixed-site hygiene, innermost first).
