## Milestone (2026-08-29 ~07:40): std lowers end to end
std root 41 (probe-param14, chain flags: `--cfg backtrace_in_libstd`, hashbrown/rustc_demangle `--dep-cfg`s, `STD_ENV_ARCH=x86_64`): graph errors 0, imports 0, body-expand failed=2 (debug_assert_matches; addr_of_mut fixed after), **HIR errors 0** over 58,766 items / 36,323 bodies; tyck typed 35,410 (partial 908, skipped 5, error nodes 500); u-MIR built 35,410; MIR lowered 34,508 (blocked 902); `--emit-umir-c` wrote /tmp/cmrustc-std41.c. Next: the std hello-world executable (scratchpad/std-hello.rs over the full chain with `--with-dep std`).
- Committed as `09b1dd50` (lanes 95, census 61 at 22,914, gates 82): the probe/u-MIR/codegen/test half of batch 3 — the `src/hir/lower.c` half was set aside by an over-broad stash and follows in the next commit together with `format_args_nl` and decl-2.0 dependency macros (lanes 96 green; census 62 / gates 83 pending).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung): the std hello-world now emits 582 KB of C that compiles and links (`println!` → `format_args_nl` supported), then segfaults in alloc's `into_vec` — `Box::into_raw_with_allocator` was a stub: its body's `ptr::read(&b.1)` on `ManuallyDrop<Box<T, A>>` failed tyck ("tuple field not found") because tuple-field access, unlike named fields, never applied a user `Deref`. Mirrored the named-field behaviour (scratch repro scratchpad/rawbox.rs over core+alloc).
- Follow-up (codegen half): with the body typed, the u-MIR `FIELD` render still took a tuple index verbatim and read slot 1 of the *wrapper* — `ManuallyDrop` is transparent, so `.1` rendered as a zero-sized read (`0`), and a `.0` on a named-field transparent wrapper matched the representative instead of descending. `cm_umir_c_field_carrier` now finds the aggregate that owns the field: the peeled base, or — when the tuple index is not one of a tuple-form aggregate's own (`cm_umir_c_aggregate_field_count`, 0 for unit/named forms) or the name is absent — the transparent wrapper's field type it derefs to (`cm_umir_c_transparent_inner`, the substituted step factored out of `cm_umir_c_representation`); the slot is read from the same block since the wrapper's value is the field's. Used by FIELD/REF_FIELD and STORE_FIELD. Fixture `tuple-field-deref` (a `Wrap { inner: Pair(u32, u32) }` with `Deref<Target = Pair>`; `w.1 + w.0` through it = 11); fixtures 94/94 gcc+tcc (probe-param17).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): with `into_raw_with_allocator` real, the hello binary crashed one frame up, in `into_vec`'s `b as *mut T`. Two causes. (1) `vec![x, 2]` is `into_vec(box_new([x, 2]))`: the `Box<[T; 2]> -> Box<[T]>` coercion was accepted argument-wise by tyck (the M9 unsize leniency) but never recorded, so no `UNSIZE` was emitted and `into_vec` read a thin box as a fat one. The ADT-argument-wise coercion now records the outer type as the site's unsize target when a child is `[T; N] -> [T]` (the `&ADT<[T; N]> -> &ADT<[T]>` reference branch suppresses the site during its recursion, since the pointee's unsize is not the reference's); C emission resolves the UNSIZE target/source through `cm_umir_c_representation`, so a pointer-shaped ADT (`Box` -> `Unique` -> `NonNull` -> `*const [T]`) takes the `[block, N]` pair path. (2) `&raw mut **b` on `ManuallyDrop<Box<T, A>>`: the explicit `*` on a `Deref` ADT was lowered as a raw load through the wrapper's value. `cm_umir_emit_user_deref` now borrows the operand's place and emits `DEREF_CALL` (`deref_mut` for a `&mut`/`&raw mut` reborrow), the unary deref loading through the returned reference; `Box` keeps its built-in deref (its own `Deref` impl is `&**self`). Fixtures `deref-unary` (`&**w`, `(*w).1` on a `Deref` wrapper = 111) and `box-unsize` (`Box<[u32; 3]>` passed as `Box<[u32]>`, `s.len() + s[1]` = 10).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): the hello binary then reached `println!` → `_print` → `OUTPUT_CAPTURE.try_with(..)` and called garbage through `LocalKey.inner`. `thread_local!` builds the key as `LocalKey::new(const { if needs_drop::<T>() { |init| .. } else { |init| .. } })`: capture-free closures where a `fn` pointer is expected. Tyck accepted `CLOSURE -> FN_PTR` silently and the closure value rendered as its environment (the frame). Now (1) the coercion is recorded in `unsize_targets` (the site-keyed record ulower already turns into `UNSIZE`); (2) every closure body gets a `<symbol>_fp(p0, ..)` thunk supplying a scratch frame as `env`, and the UNSIZE render for FN_PTR ← CLOSURE is the thunk's address (the closure instanced as a call through the value would instance it); (3) `if`/`match` branches typed against a fn-pointer `expected` are coerced at the branch site each (two closures never join), the expression taking the expected type. Fixture `closure-fnptr` (let-annotated, argument-position, and `if`-branch closures as `fn(u32) -> u32` = 32); fixtures 97/97 gcc (probe-param20).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): with the key's fn pointer real, the closure body `|init| { #[thread_local] static VAL: LazyStorage<..> = ..; VAL.get(init, __init) }` rendered `VAL` as `0 /* item path */`: body-local items were reserved only under `fn` bodies, and the descent through a body stopped at blocks/if/match/loops/closures — a call argument (`LocalKey::new(const { .. })`) was never entered. Static and const initializers now reserve their body items (both the module-level and the body-local reservation paths, via `cm_lower_reserve_items_in_expr`), and the descent also covers call/method-call operands, field/index bases, unary/binary/cast/try/raw-ref operands, return/break values and tuple/array elements. Tyck side: call arguments are pre-typed without expectation except closures; an `if`/`match`/block whose value position is a closure (`cm_tyck_yields_closure`) is deferred the same way, so the branch closures learn their parameter types from the fn-pointer parameter. Fixture `closure-static` (= 43); fixtures 97/97 (probe-param22).
- Follow-up: std's `VAL` then had no u-MIR body — its initializer is `__INIT`, a `const` declared in the *enclosing* `{{ const __INIT: $t = $init; .. }}` block of the key's initializer, and a body-local item's own body started with an empty nested-item table. ubody now snapshots the nested table each block registers (per declared item, keyed by source + AST item; `CmUNestedScope`, owners lower before the items they declare) and a body-local item's body starts from its snapshot. Fixture `closure-static` now declares `VAL` from `__INIT`; fixtures 98/98 (probe-param23).
- Follow-up: the other branch's closure, `|_| { static VAL: $t = __INIT; &VAL }`, reaches its static only through `&VAL` (`STATIC_ADDR`), which rendered the `_addr()` call without registering the static as a program instance — so nothing emitted it (not even a stub) and the unit failed to link. `STATIC_ADDR` now registers the instance like a value read does. Fixtures 98/98 (probe-param24).
- ASan lane caught a heap-use-after-free in `cm_umir_c_transparent_inner` (box-unsize): the wrapper's `CmTy *` was read for its arguments after `cm_ty_from_hir` had grown the arena; the arguments are copied out first now, and the UNSIZE render computes both representations before holding any `CmTy *` (the type-arena realloc lesson again). Fixtures 98/98 under the sanitized probe.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): the hello binary then reached `print_to` → `write_fmt` → `default_write_fmt` → `Adapter::write_str` → `write_all` → `self.write(buf)`, a "declaration without body" stub: every instance on that path lacked its Self (`print_to BARE [T]`, `write_fmt []`, `write_all []`). Root: `print_to(args, stdout, "stdout")` takes `global_s: fn() -> T`, and the `FN_DEF -> FN_PTR` coercion accepted the fn item without unifying its signature with the pointer's, so `T` was never inferred as `Stdout`. `cm_tyck_fn_def_signature` (the instance construction shared with `cm_tyck_call`) now gives the item's parameter/return types and the coercion unifies them with the pointer's. Fixture `trait-default-self` (provided method → generic helper → generic adapter impl → provided method → required method, through a `fn() -> T` argument = 104); fixtures 98/98 (probe-param25).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): with Self flowing, the hello binary hung: `impl Write for Stdout { fn write_fmt(&mut self, args) { (&*self).write_fmt(args) } }` called itself. Tyck found `<&Stdout as Write>::write_fmt` at step 0, then `cm_tyck_prefer_pointee` (by-value before autoref: a `&self` method of T beats one of `&T`) swapped in `Stdout`'s own `write_fmt` — a `&mut self` method that is out of reach behind the shared `&Stdout`. The preference now skips a `&mut self` alternative when the reference is shared. Fixture `ref-impl-pick` (= 103); fixtures 99/99 (probe-param26).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): with the print path real (C 600 → 732 KB), the unit failed to link on `errno_location`: std declares it `#[cfg_attr(target_os = "linux", link_name = "__errno_location")]` and the foreign wrappers used the Rust name. `cm_umir_c_item_link_name` reads a `link_name = ".."` attribute (cfg_attr is already expanded by then) and both the foreign fn and foreign static wrappers bind to it. Fixture `link-name` (fn and static through link names = 43); fixtures 101/101 (probe-param27).
- Follow-up: honoring link names surfaced core_arch's `#[link_name = "llvm.x86.sse2.pause"] fn pause()` (`_mm_pause`), not a C identifier; a foreign fn whose link name is an LLVM intrinsic (contains `.`) is a no-op shim returning 0. Fixtures 101/101 (probe-param28).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): the hello binary (now fully linked) crashed in `Stdout::lock` on a null `inner`: `stdout()` fills it from `STDOUT.get_or_init(..)`, a stub — `OnceLock::get_or_init` is `match self.get_or_try_init(|| Ok::<T, !>(f())) { Ok(val) => val }` and the turbofish on the variant path was ignored (`cm_tyck_adt_fresh` alone), leaving `E` unresolved. `cm_tyck_bind_path_adt_args` binds an ADT instance's type generics to the explicit arguments on the path's last segment (or the enum segment before it) for variant and tuple-struct constructor paths. Core: 22,936 → 22,941 typed, error nodes 113. Fixture `never-turbofish` (= 4).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `get_or_init` real, `get_or_try_init` next: `self.initialize(f)?` typed `Result<(), ?E>` — the callee's own `E` is reachable only by matching its `F: FnOnce() -> Result<T, E>` bound against the caller's bound on the `F` it passes. `cm_tyck_unify_fn_bounds` (call and method-call argument sites): when the argument's type is a generic parameter with an Fn-family bound in the current item and the callee's parameter has one, the two bound signatures (built by `cm_tyck_closure_expectation` for each side) unify, undone on mismatch. Fixture `bound-infer` (callee result dropped, `E` bound-only; partial + crash before, = 14/210 after); fixtures 102/102 (probe-param30).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `get_or_try_init` real, the next stub was the futex `Once::call(&self, _, f: &mut dyn FnMut(&OnceState))`: `f(&f_state)` typed `<dyn FnMut<(&OnceState,)> as FnOnce>::Output` and never normalized — the DYN `CmTy` kept only the principal's arguments, dropping the HIR's `Output = ()` equality. Three pieces: (1) `cm_ty_dyn` carries the equalities' values after the principal's arguments (`a` = their start, `def2` = the first bound associated type) and the normalizer resolves a projection on a DYN self to that binding; (2) a callee autoderefs (`&mut dyn FnMut`, `&F`) before the call-kind checks; (3) codegen: a call through a `dyn Fn*` value packs its arguments into one tuple block and dispatches through the pair's vtable at the principal's `call*` slot (`cm_umir_c_dyn_call_method`), and a closure's vtable Fn-family entries are a synthesized `<closure>_vt(self_ref, args)` thunk (env = the value behind the data pointer, parameters unpacked from the tuple). Fixture `dyn-fn-output` (`&mut |v| ..` as `&mut dyn FnMut(u32) -> u32`, called twice = 27); fixtures 103/103 (probe-param33). std tyck 35,473 typed.
- Follow-up: with the binding carried, `Once::call`'s `f(&f_state)` typed as a bare inference variable — `cm_tyck_call`'s DYN callee branch typed "loosely". It now takes the parameters from the principal's tuple argument and the result from the object's `Output` binding (falling back to loose typing without one). Fixtures 104/104 (probe-param34).
- Follow-up: the unresolved node was still `f(&f_state)` (source 674, 6465..6476): `dyn FnMut(&OnceState)` has no `-> R`, so the HIR carries no `Output` equality (rustc defaults it to `()`), and the DYN branch required one. Without a binding an Fn-family object's call is `()`. `dyn-fn-output` gains a `&mut dyn FnMut(u32)` callee (= 34); fixtures 104/104 (probe-param35).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): the stdout lock now succeeds; `LineWriterShim::write_all` then called core's `memrchr`, a stub: `type Chunk = usize;` declared inside the fn body was invisible to type paths (`text.align_to::<(Chunk, Chunk)>()`, `*const Chunk`) — module resolution never sees body-local items and body-local `type` aliases were not even reserved. A single-segment type path now consults the body's own item statements for a `type` alias (tyck's `cm_tyck_ast_path_type`), and the body-item reservation includes type aliases. Fixture `local-type-alias` (alias in a turbofish, a cast and a `let`; a method on `(x as Chunk)` stays unresolved without it; = 110); fixtures 105/105 (probe-param36).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `memrchr` real, it crashed on `text[offset..].iter()`: a range index was lowered as an *element* index (`RangeFrom` is transparent, so its value is the start), and `&text[offset..]` as that element's address. New u-MIR rvalue `SUBSLICE` (immediate = range form: from/to/range/full/inclusive/to-inclusive), emitted by the INDEX value case, `cm_umir_address_of` and the REF-of-INDEX branch when the index's type is one of core's `ops::Range*` structs (`cm_umir_range_form`); C emission builds a fresh [data + start, end - start] pair over the base's elements (scalar elements at their width, others as slots; end from the pair's length or the array type). Fixture `range-index` (`xs[n..]`, `&xs[a..b]`, `&xs[..b]` over a `&[u32]` = 133); fixtures 106/106 (probe-param37).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `memrchr` returned; `LineWriterShim::flush_if_completed_line` then read `self.buffer().last().copied()` and core's `<[T]>::last` — `if let [.., last] = self { Some(last) } else { None }` — returned `Some(null)`: (1) a bare `..` is not an element of the u-body's slice-pattern list (`rest_index` counts the elements before it), yet tyck and the lowering's shape helper treated the element *at* `rest_index` as the rest (typing `last` as the slice, counting nothing after the rest); (2) elements after the rest were never extracted — INDEX/REF_INDEX now carry `immediate = k` meaning `len - k` and both the test and binding walks visit the tail (`cm_umir_slice_pattern_tail`); (3) `if let` / `while let` (`cm_umir_emit_pattern_test`) only switched on an enum discriminant — any other refutable shape (slice length, literal, range, nested variant) was treated as irrefutable; it now runs the match-arm pattern checks with every failure edge landing in the `else` block. Fixture `slice-pat-tail` (`[.., last]`, `[first, .., last]`, `[.., a, _]`, `[only]`, `[]` on `&[u32]` incl. empty/short fallbacks = 8351); fixtures 107/107 (probe-param38).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `"hello "` reached the LineWriter; formatting the argument crashed in `StdoutLock::write_all`'s `self.inner.borrow_mut().write_all(buf)` — the *second* `borrow_mut()` returned null: no destructor ever ran, so the first `RefMut`'s borrow flag stayed set, `try_borrow_mut` failed and the panic fell through a `-> !` stub that returned. Two pieces. (1) Drop glue for receiver temporaries: a method-call receiver produced by a call whose callee borrows it (`&self` / `&mut self`) dies after the call; ulower emits `DROP` (new rvalue) and C emission renders glue — the type's own `<T as Drop>::drop` on the slot's address, then each struct field's glue (block slot i; a transparent wrapper's field is the value), enums/pointers not walked (`cm_umir_c_type_needs_drop` gates it). (2) A stub for a `-> !` fn calls `abort()` instead of returning 0. Fixture `temp-drop` (a guard with the Drop on a field, taken twice as a temporary receiver: borrows back to 0, 2 drops = 20207; wrong on the old probe); fixtures 108/108 (probe-param39).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): with the RefCell guard dropped, the argument's `memrchr` crashed in `text.align_to::<(Chunk, Chunk)>()` — its `(self, &[], &[])` early return coerces tuple-wise, and each element's `&[T; 0] -> &[U]` unsize was recorded at the *tuple's* site, so C emission unsized the whole tuple local. `cm_tyck_coerce_at` now follows a block to its tail and coerces a tuple expression element by element at each element's own site (the generic tuple branch suppresses the site); `if` / `match` branches typed against a tuple expectation coerce per branch like fn pointers (two tuples with differently-sized empty arrays never join). Fixture `tuple-unsize` (`(xs, &[], &[])` / `(&[], xs, &[])` per branch = 30300; partial + crash before); fixtures 109/109 (probe-param40).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `align_to` fixed, the LineWriter's `Some(newline_idx)` arm reached `BufWriter::flush_buf`, a stub: it declares `struct BufGuard` with its own `impl` blocks inside the fn body and calls `BufGuard::new(&mut self.buf)` — tyck's NESTED_ITEM case typed a body-local struct as a fresh variable ("no HIR definition to name yet"), leaving the associated path and every later use unresolved. Body-local structs/enums/unions are body_local HIR items now: the path is re-typed as `TYPE_ASSOC` on the item (`Guard::new`) or as its constructor (`Guard(..)`). Fixture `local-impl` (a body-local struct with inherent methods and a Drop impl, driven through `Guard::new` = 47; 7 unresolved nodes before); fixtures 109/109 (probe-param41).
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): `flush_buf` real, `self.inner.write(guard.remaining())` passed a null slice: `remaining` is `&self.buffer[self.written..]` on a `Vec<u8>`, and the range index on an ADT base rendered `0` (SUBSLICE only knew slice/array bases). Tyck already types such an index through the ADT's `Deref` (Vec's Index impls delegate to the slice); lowering now does the same: an index on a Vec-like ADT base derefs first (`Deref::deref(&base)` as a `DEREF_CALL` to `&[T]` / `&str`, reference layers loaded down to one; `Box` keeps its built-in deref) and the slice is indexed or subsliced — in the value, address-of and reborrow forms. Fixture `vec-index` (a Deref-to-slice struct: `b[n..].len()`, `&b[n..]`, `b[i]` = 4205; crash before); fixtures 111/111 (probe-param42).
- MILESTONE (2026-08-29, probe-param42): the std hello world's `println!` runs end to end down to the syscall — strace shows `write(0, "hello hi 0\n", 11)`. Three defects visible in that line: the fd is 0 (`FileDesc::from_raw_fd` is `Self(FromRawFd::from_raw_fd(raw_fd))` and the `Self(..)` tuple-struct constructor rendered `0 /* call */`), the second argument prints 0 (`v.len()` — to be traced), and the EBADF path then crashes on the `Err(ref e)` binding. Fixed here: a CALL whose callee is the `Self` type path in a tuple struct's impl is the struct's constructor (aggregate of the arguments), like the by-name form. Fixture `ctor-call` (`Self(OwnedFd::from_raw_fd(fd))` vs `FileDesc(..)` = 313; `0 /* call */` before); fixtures 111/111 (probe-param43).
- Regression + fix (2026-08-29): the `Self(..)` constructor rule matched `Self::new_unchecked(align)` (a `Self` path *with* a further segment whose result type is the tuple struct `Alignment`), turning the call into an aggregate; the alloc probes segfaulted (gates 106 alloc 0/2) — and commit 66582328 went out anyway because the commit script did not check the gate's result. The rule now requires the bare single-segment `Self`; alloc probes pass again on probe-param45. Lesson: gate scripts must fail the shell (`grep -q 'fail=0'`) before a commit command runs in the same line.
- Measured pass (M9-06, std hello rung, continued): the stdout write succeeds with fd 1 once `Self(..)` lowers; the remaining `hello hi 0` (`v.len()` = 0) traced to `vec![x, 2]`'s `Box<[u32; 2]> -> Box<[u32]>` unsize falling to the incomplete path: `cm_umir_c_transparent_field` tested a struct's *raw* HIR field types, so `Box<T, A>`'s `alloc: A` was the bare parameter `A` (never zero-sized) and no `Box` was ever transparent. It now substitutes the ADT's arguments (`Global` is a ZST), which makes `Box<T, Global>` its `Unique` pointer; with that, the address of a transparent wrapper's zero-sized non-representative field is the base's address rather than 0 (`ptr::read(&b.1)` on the allocator dereferences it). (The commit 52cf58c7 title mentions this change; it lands with the next commit.)

## MILESTONE (2026-08-29, probe-param47): the Rust 1.90.0 std hello world runs
scratchpad/std-hello.rs -- `String::from("hi")`, `vec![x, 2]`, `println!("hello {} {}", s, v.len())`, `s.len() + v.len()` -- compiled by the lenient M9 pipeline over real core + alloc + std (with cfg_if, libc, unwind, panic_abort, panic_unwind, hashbrown, std_detect, rustc_demangle) into 838 KB of C99, built with gcc, prints `hello hi 2` on fd 1 and returns 4. Every layer of std's stdout stack runs: `OnceLock::get_or_init` (futex `Once::call` through `&mut dyn FnMut`), `ReentrantLock` + `Tid`, `RefCell` borrow guards dropped as temporaries, `LineWriterShim` and `BufWriter::flush_buf` (body-local `BufGuard`), core's `memrchr` and `align_to`, `fmt::write` through `dyn Write` vtables, `FileDesc::write` to libc `write`. Frontier stubs still present (not on this path): do_panic x2, futex_wait, futex_wake, abort_on_dtor_unwind, register, new, mod_inv, id, eq, count, compiletime, debug_assert_fd_is_open, next x3, addr x3, clone x2, write, align_of_val. Gates: lanes 119/120, census 88 (23,057 typed, 22,628 lowered, 120 error nodes), core 3/3, alloc 2/2. Next rungs: a real `fn main` entry (std's `lang_start`), then rustc's own crates toward the self-compile fixpoint.
- Measured pass (M9-06, standalone binary rung): the emitted C now carries a program entry: the root crate (the crate compiled last, highest crate id) with a parameterless, parentless `fn main` is rooted like a `#[no_mangle]` export and gets `int main(void) { cm_u_<main>(); return 0; }` (std's `lang_start` runtime setup is skipped for now -- `println!` did not need it). scratchpad/hello-main.rs (`fn main() { let v = vec![1, 2, 3]; let name = String::from("world"); println!("Hello, {}! {}", name, v.len()); }`) links against scratchpad/std-hello-shims.c (the runtime hooks: `__rust_alloc`, panic entry points) with no C harness.
- Measured pass (M9-06, standalone binary rung, continued): `hello-main.rs` typed `String::from` as unresolved: the compiler-injected prelude was synthesized from the *first* registered dependency's `prelude::rust_2024` (core's, without `String` / `Vec`). It now takes the outermost dependency's prelude -- the one registered last (std over alloc over core), as rustc injects the facade's; alloc has no `prelude::rust_2024` so core+alloc roots keep core's.

## MILESTONE (2026-08-29, probe-param50): a plain `fn main` Rust program is a standalone binary
scratchpad/hello-main.rs -- `fn main() { let v = vec![1, 2, 3]; let name = String::from("world"); println!("Hello, {}! {}", name, v.len()); }` with no attributes and no C harness -- compiles through core/alloc/std into C, links against the runtime hooks only (scratchpad/std-hello-shims.c: `__rust_alloc` and the panic entry points) and prints `Hello, world! 3` with exit code 0. std's `lang_start` runtime setup is still skipped (the C `main` calls the Rust `main` directly). Gates: lanes 122, census 90 (23,057 typed), core 3/3, alloc 2/2, std-hello harness still `hello hi 2`.
- Measured pass (M9-06, standalone binary rung, continued): the C entry now calls std's `#[lang = "start"] lang_start::<()>(&main, argc, argv, 0)` (found by its lang attribute, instanced for `T = ()` at seeding) so args, the panic path and the exit code go through the real runtime; a program without std keeps the direct `main()` call. Next probe program: scratchpad/std-stress.rs (env::args, HashMap, iterators + closures, Result/?, {:?}, format!, assert_eq!).
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param53): with the entry wired, `lang_start` itself was a stub: its body is `lang_start_internal(&move || __rust_begin_short_backtrace(main).report().to_i32(), ..)`, and `__rust_begin_short_backtrace<F: FnOnce() -> T, T>(f: F)` receives `main: fn() -> T` -- `cm_tyck_unify_fn_bounds` only matched a caller *bound* against the callee's `Fn*` bound, so a fn-pointer (or fn-item) argument never told the callee its `T`. It now takes `FN_PTR` (the pointer's own signature) and `FN_DEF` (through `cm_tyck_fn_def_signature`) actuals as the signature to unify with the bound. Fixture `fnptr-bound` (a `fn() -> u32` and a fn item passed to `F: FnOnce() -> T`, result dropped so `T` is reachable only through the bound) fails on param52 and passes on param53; the hello-main chain then emits `lang_start` as a real instance (#1064), no longer a stub.
- Measured pass (M9-06, tyck, probe-param53): `let v: Vec<i64> = ...` typed as `Vec<i64, ?>`: an annotated type path with fewer arguments than parameters left the rest to inference and the allocator never resolved when the value came from a generic producer. `cm_tyck_ast_path_type` now unifies each omitted trailing type parameter with its declared default (`A = Global`), substituted through the instance so `T = Self`-style defaults resolve; a bare path (`Vec::new()`) still infers everything. Fixture `param-default` (`Wrap<T, A = Global>` produced by `fn make<A: Mk>() -> Wrap<u32, A>`, `A` reachable only through the default) fails on param52, passes on param53.
- Measured pass (M9-06, tyck, probe-param53): `panic_output() -> Option<impl io::Write>` (std's `rtprintpanic!`, on the `lang_start_internal` / `signal_handler` / `init` path) typed every use as a fresh variable: `impl Trait` in a return position had no hidden type. `cm_tyck_body` now records, for a function whose declared return type mentions `impl Trait` anywhere (`cm_tyck_hir_type_has_opaque`), the deeply-resolved return type once the body is typed (`CmTyckState.hidden`, def -> type); `cm_tyck_signature` substitutes that hidden type for callers, typing the callee body on demand when the caller is checked first (guarded against recursion with a none entry; the scratch `CmTyckBody` is released, and the swapped per-crate graph/imports/modules restored). Fixture `opaque-return` (caller declared before `fn maker(flag) -> Opt<impl Tag>`, the hidden `Two` consumed through `.tag()`) fails on param52, passes on param53.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param54): the hello-main unit then failed to *compile*: libc's `pub fn abort() -> !` was wrapped as `long long cm_u_4_208(void) { long long abort(); return (long long)abort(); }`, conflicting with the prelude's own `void abort(void);`. Foreign wrappers now (a) forward the prelude-prototyped hosts (`abort`, `malloc`, `calloc`, `memmove`, `memset`) with their C types (`cm_umir_c_render_prelude_foreign`) and (b) declare a never-returning foreign fn (`-> !`, e.g. `exit`) as `void NAME()` and trap after the call. Fixture `foreign-never` (`extern "C" { fn abort() -> !; fn exit(status: i32) -> !; }`, the harness exits through `exit(0)`) fails on param53 (`conflicting types for 'abort'`), passes on param54.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param55): `lang_start_internal` runs `panic::catch_unwind(..)` -> `panicking::try` -> `intrinsics::catch_unwind(do_call::<F, R>, data_ptr, do_catch::<F, R>)`, a bodyless intrinsic and so a stub. Without unwinding the semantics are "run the try fn, return 0": an intrinsic shim now calls the try fn pointer with the data pointer and returns 0 (the catch fn is never reached). Fixture `catch-shim` (`#[rustc_intrinsic] unsafe fn catch_unwind(..)` with a try fn that bumps the pointee) fails on param54, passes on param55. Remaining on that path per the param53 log: `do_call` (unresolved 4, errors 2), `do_catch` (errors 1), `init` (errors 1), `signal_handler` (unresolved 9, errors 1) -- next.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param56): with the shim in place the binary still printed nothing: `do_call` / `do_catch` were partial (`data.f`, `data.p` on `&mut u8`): `union Data<F, R>` is declared inside `try`'s body and the sibling body-local fns cast their `*mut u8` to `*mut Data<F, R>` -- a type path naming an item of the *enclosing* body, which module resolution never sees and the body's own `STMT_ITEM` scan (same body only) misses. HIR items now carry `body_owner` (the outermost enclosing fn, from the lowering record); tyck indexes body-local items by owner (`CmTyckState.body_items`, sorted like `children`) and single-segment type paths consult the current body's owner (or the fn itself) before the generics. The ADT/alias path typing moved into `cm_tyck_adt_path_type` / `cm_tyck_alias_path_type` so both lookups share the explicit-argument and default handling. Fixture `sibling-item` (the union built in `run`, read through `*mut u8` in sibling `do_call<T>`) fails on param55 (`field not found`), passes on param56. Lesson recorded while writing it: the first fixture type-punned a `Pair` slot as the union and crashed -- nested aggregates live behind block pointers, so punning across aggregate types is outside the representation; std's use (same type both sides) is fine.
- Measured pass (M9-06, tyck, probe-param57): `init`'s `set_current_info(range, Some(Box::from("main")))` typed the argument `Option<Box<&str>>` against `Option<Box<str>>`: `From<T> for Box<T>` and `From<&str> for Box<str>` both accept `&str`, and the inner call never saw the expectation because call arguments are pre-typed before the callee's parameters are known (that pre-typing feeds the overlapping-impl retry). Two rungs: (a) in the assoc-fn retry (`Type::from(..)`), with an expectation the first pass accepts only a candidate whose result fits it once the arguments are bound (undo-marked), a second pass takes the first argument-compatible one as before; an expectation-chosen non-first impl is pinned through `method_targets`; (b) when a call argument fails to coerce to its parameter, a call-expression argument is typed once more with the parameter as expectation before the mismatch counts (`Some(..)` re-typed with `Option<Box<str>>` in turn re-types `Box::from(..)` with `Box<str>`). Fixture `expect-impl` (`Bx::from(u32)` into `Opt<Bx<u64>>` and `Bx<u64>` slots, the specific impl doubling) fails on param56, passes on param57.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param57): with `do_call` typed, gdb showed the param56 binary reaching `lang_start_internal` -> `catch_unwind` -> the shim -> `do_call`, and exiting there: `f()` had rendered as `0 /* call */` because the `do_call` instance was `[?, ?]` -- `try`'s `catch_unwind(do_call::<F, R>, data_ptr, do_catch::<F, R>)` names a body-local fn, and the `NESTED_ITEM` value path built its FN_DEF straight from the HIR item, dropping the turbofish (the `fn(*mut u8)` parameter mentions neither generic). The nested-fn value path now re-enters `cm_tyck_path_type` as a `DEFINITION` path (as body-local structs already did), so the turbofish binds the generics and the target is recorded. Fixture `nested-turbofish` (`inner::<T>` taken as a `fn(*mut u8) -> u32` inside a generic fn, the callee casting back to `*mut T`) fails on param56 (typed clean, but `tag()` vanishes and the harness sees the wrong sum), passes on param57.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param58): with `do_call` real the binary ran `main`'s runtime setup and crashed in `get_stack_start` (`init` -> `sys::init` -> `stack_overflow::init` -> `install_main_guard_linux` -> `stack_start_aligned`): `assert_eq!(libc::pthread_attr_getstack(..), 0)` expands to `match (&a, &b) { (left_val, right_val) => .. }`, and an irrefutable tuple (or `&pat`) pattern as a *match arm* fell through every branch of the arm dispatch -- no bindings at all, so `*left_val` read address 0. Such arms now go through the irrefutable binder (`cm_umir_bind_pattern`). Fixture `tuple-arm` (`match (x, 10) { (a, b) => a + b }` and the by-reference `assert_eq!` shape) fails on param57 (segfault), passes on param58.
- Contract + fix (2026-08-29, probe-param58): the fixed fixture still returned garbage through the plain `gcc` of the nix shell: that wrapper defaults to `-O2` (plus stack-protector/clash hardening), and the emitted C reads `long long` slots back through `int32_t *` / `uint32_t *` / pointer types -- strict-aliasing UB that `-O2` exploits (`-O0`, `-O1`, MSan and an inserted `fprintf` all "fixed" it). The unit now opens with `#pragma GCC optimize("no-strict-aliasing")`, and every driver that compiles emitted C passes `-fno-strict-aliasing` (fixture/core/alloc runners via `EMIT_CFLAGS`, skipped for tcc; the std chain script). Both fixture censuses 121/121 on param58 (gcc, tcc).
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param59/60): the param58 binary aborted in `pthread_attr_destroy`: `get_stack_start` hands libc `attr.as_mut_ptr()` for a `MaybeUninit<libc::pthread_attr_t>`, and a libc struct is a slot block here, so C writes 56 bytes over the slot region. C-layout (`#[repr(C)]` / libc) structs passed to the host are a representation milestone of their own (std's `stat64` / `dirent64` / `sigaction` / `pollfd` all need it; see the C-layout follow-up below). For the runtime rung the emitter gained `CMRUSTC_UMIR_FORCE_STUB="module::fn,.."` (matched on the item's owner-module name + name, reason `forced`), and the std chain runs with `stack_overflow::init,unix::sanitize_standard_fds` stubbed (`reset_sigpipe` and `args::init` stay real).
- Measured pass (M9-06, tyck/parse, probe-param59): `signal_handler` was partial on a Rust 2024 **let chain**: `if let Some(info) = info && info.guard_page_range.contains(..)` -- `cm_parser_parse_if` parsed the whole `info && ..` as the if-let scrutinee. `cm_parser_parse_let_condition` (shared by `if let` / `while let`) now parses the scrutinee at the `let`-expression binding power (stopping before `&&`) and, on `&&`, builds the condition as `LET(pat, e) && ..` (the infix continuation `cm_parser_parse_expression_from` runs on the pre-parsed `LET`; later `&& let ..` operands come from the prefix `let` form) with no pattern on the if/while node: tyck's `LET` case binds the pattern, ulower's `LET` case tests it. That exposed `&&` / `||` lowering as *eager* (both operands evaluated, then the boolean op): the chain read the unbound `info` after a failed `LET`. Logical operators now short-circuit (SWITCH_BOOL on the left operand; the right operand's block writes the result, the other writes the constant). Fixture `let-chain` (`if let .. && x > 0 && let ..`, `while let .. && n > 0`) fails on param58, passes on param60.
- Measured pass (M9-06, patterns, probe-param60): `match self` in a `&self` method (`Opt::Some(v) => *v`) crashed twice over: (a) the SWITCH terminator read the discriminant from the reference slot itself (the payload slots already loaded through the reference) -- the render now derefs a `&Enum` / `*const Enum` scrutinee once; (b) the payload binding held the slot *value* while `*v` needs `&u32`: tyck had no default binding modes (it peeled the scrutinee's reference and typed the bindings by value; `*v` only typed by leniency). Under a peeled `&`/`&mut`, tuple and struct/tuple-struct sub-patterns are now matched against `&Field` / `&mut Field` (`cm_tyck_pat_expect`), so bindings become references and nested patterns peel again; ulower reads such payloads with the new `CM_UMIR_RVALUE_REF_SLOT` (the slot's address; also for explicit `ref` bindings) through `cm_umir_payload_rvalue` at every payload site (checks/bind for tuple, tuple-struct and struct patterns, and the match-arm fast paths). Fixture `match-ref` (`peek(&self)` / `is_some(&self)` on `Some(7)` and `None`) fails on param59 (segfault), passes on param60; both fixture censuses 123/123.
- Follow-up milestone (C-layout aggregates): structs from `libc` / `#[repr(C)]` with scalar, array and nested-C fields need a byte-exact block (`calloc(size)`, typed field access at real offsets, references = the block pointer) so std can pass `pthread_attr_t`, `sigaction`, `stack_t`, `pollfd[3]`, `stat64`, `dirent64`, `timespec`, `iovec` to the host. Not started.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param64): generic
  arguments written on an in-scope trait predicate are now bound to the
  selected trait instance before its method is freshened.  This types
  `R: RangeBounds<usize>` methods as `Bound<&usize>` instead of `Bound<&?>`
  and restores the real `core::slice::range` path used by `Vec::drain`.
  Fixture `pattern-generic` covers a generic trait argument flowing through
  a returned enum reference and an inherent method.  Whole-core census:
  23,158 typed / 219 partial, 345 unresolved nodes, 111 error nodes;
  22,701 u-MIR-lowered / 457 blocked.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param67): u-MIR now
  emits conservative direct-RAII drops for lexical let bindings on normal
  fall-through and return paths.  This runs std's `CompletionGuard::drop`,
  allowing futex `Once` to transition from RUNNING to COMPLETE after runtime
  initialization and cleanup.  General move-path flags and break/continue
  cleanup remain future work; `Vec` scope drops stay excluded.  The compiler
  also treats `#[lang = "drop_in_place"]` as compiler glue rather than
  recursing into its deliberate Rust placeholder body.  Fixture `named-drop`
  covers fall-through cleanup.
- Measured pass (M9-06, real `lang_start` rung, probe-param67): Rust allocator
  wrappers use the emitter's one-word allocation header convention for
  `__rust_alloc`, `__rust_alloc_zeroed`, `__rust_realloc`, and
  `__rust_dealloc`, so boxed arrays transferred into `Vec` are deallocated
  from their actual allocation base.  Final runtime gate: the plain
  `hello-main.rs` enters through real `lang_start`, prints exactly
  `Hello, world! 3`, completes std cleanup, and exits 0 (only
  `stack_overflow::imp::init` and `unix::sanitize_standard_fds` remain forced
  stubs pending C-layout aggregates).  Final gates: GCC, strict Clang,
  TinyCC, and GCC ASan/UBSan/leak lanes each pass all 125 executable fixtures;
  core-linked 3/3 and alloc-linked 2/2.

- Measured pass (M9-06, std stress rung, probe-param88): a real Rust 1.90
  program now runs through `lang_start`, argument collection, tokenization,
  `String` formatting/comparison, `HashMap` insertion/iteration, sorting,
  ranges, iterator adapters, and normal cleanup.  The final nondeterministic
  UTF-8 abort was an inconsistent bootstrap `String` layout: the
  `str -> String` specialization retained a `Unique` wrapper even though
  transparent ADTs are flattened everywhere else.  Its raw Vec data slot now
  holds the byte pointer directly, while `String::{as_str,as_bytes}` build a
  fat descriptor from that pointer and the live length.  A fresh full-std
  artifact passes 20/20 repeated runs and its six-argument `process::exit(3)`
  branch.  Gates: native suite, 136/136 executable u-MIR fixtures,
  core-linked 3/3, and alloc-linked 2/2.  The two C-layout-dependent runtime
  functions remain the only forced std startup stubs.  Next: enter rustc's
  own crate graph and drive the first compiler binary to a self-hosted
  fixpoint.

- Measured pass (M9-06, first compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_lexer` now runs as a real dependency above the complete
  core/alloc/std stack and its vendored `memchr`, `unicode-xid`, and
  `unicode-properties` dependencies.  `rustc_lexer::is_ident` exercises the
  Unicode-XID tables and `Iterator::all`; the latter arrives through the
  lenient dependency artifact as a declaration-only trait default, so the C
  emitter now supplies its short-circuiting loop over the concrete
  `Iterator::next` for fn-item, fn-pointer, and closure predicates.  The
  generated 1,573,477-byte C unit has 1,732 reachable instances: 1,461 real
  bodies, 242 shims, 29 residual stubs, and 14 vtables.  It prints
  `rustc_lexer: true true false true` and exits 0 through real `lang_start`
  and cleanup.  Opening `memchr` also found that applying an alias for an
  `unsafe fn(&T, &mut U)` moved only the function-pointer container span to
  the use site while retaining its synthesized lifetime-binder spans from
  the declaration; alias normalization now preserves the enclosing span
  whenever that binder is nonempty.  Gates: native suite, 136/136 standalone
  u-MIR fixtures, core-linked 4/4 (including the new `Iterator::all`
  semantic probe), and alloc-linked 2/2.  Next: advance through the next
  low-dependency rustc crates toward the compiler binary.

- Measured pass (M9-06, second compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_graphviz` now runs as a real dependency above the complete
  core/alloc/std stack.  Its HTML escaping path exposed three semantic gaps:
  nested `TYPE_ASSOC` enum patterns retained only the enclosing enum
  resolution, so tyck now records the exact variant target for u-MIR's
  discriminant tests and payload offsets; comparison lookup selected the
  first receiver-compatible `PartialEq` impl without checking the implicitly
  borrowed right operand, so `[T] == [U]` was miscompiled through the earlier
  `[T] == [U; N]` impl and its `TryFrom`/`as_array` path; and explicit const
  turbofish values were neither carried in the `FN_DEF` instance nor rendered
  from the active monomorphization.  The generated 1,608,847-byte C unit has
  1,962 reachable instances: 1,662 real bodies, 265 shims, 35 residual stubs,
  and 19 vtables.  It prints exactly
  `rustc_graphviz: a&amp;&quot;&lt;&gt;<br align="left"/>` and exits 0 through real
  `lang_start` and cleanup.  Regressions cover nested enum variants,
  overlapping slice/array comparison impls, and concrete const-generic value
  paths.  Gates: native suite, 139/139 standalone u-MIR fixtures, core-linked
  4/4, and alloc-linked 2/2.  Next: continue through the low-dependency rustc
  crates toward the compiler binary.

- Measured pass (M9-06, third compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_error_codes` now runs as a real dependency above the
  complete core/alloc/std stack.  Its exported `error_codes!` macro expands
  the compiler's full diagnostic-code table into caller-provided items; the
  probe verifies that its first entry is `E0001: 1`.  This exposed primitive
  `&str` comparisons falling through to C descriptor-pointer equality, so
  the C emitter now compares the fat references' lengths and byte contents
  for `==` and `!=`.  The generated 1,468,471-byte C unit has 1,805 reachable
  instances: 1,525 real bodies, 249 shims, 31 residual stubs, and 15 vtables.
  It prints `rustc_error_codes: E0001 1` and exits 0 through real
  `lang_start` and cleanup.  A standalone regression compares independently
  materialized equal strings as well as unequal strings.  Gates: native
  suite, 140/140 standalone u-MIR fixtures, core-linked 4/4, and alloc-linked
  2/2.  Next: continue through the low-dependency rustc crates toward the
  compiler binary.

- Measured pass (M9-06, fourth compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_arena` now runs as a real dependency above the complete
  core/alloc/std stack and its vendored `smallvec` dependency.  Its first
  `TypedArena::alloc` exposed method lookup stopping after one user `Deref`:
  `RefMut<Vec<ArenaChunk<T>>>.last_mut()` needs the two-step chain
  `RefMut<Vec<_>> -> Vec<_> -> [_]`.  Tyck now records a bounded ordered
  receiver-deref chain and u-MIR emits each `Deref`/`DerefMut` call in turn.
  Arena teardown then exposed lexical scope cleanup omitting `RefMut`, whose
  RAII destructor lives on its `BorrowRefMut` field rather than on the outer
  type; the conservative scope-drop renderer now admits that field-owned
  guard while the wider move-path limitation remains.  Regressions cover
  two-step method autoderef and a locally bound `RefMut`-shaped guard.  The
  generated 1,374,090-byte C unit has 1,908 reachable instances: 1,610 real
  bodies, 270 shims, 28 residual stubs, and 16 vtables.  It prints
  `rustc_arena: 42`, drops the arena cleanly, and exits 0 through real
  `lang_start` and cleanup.  Gates: native suite, 142/142 standalone u-MIR
  fixtures, core-linked 4/4, and alloc-linked 2/2.  Next: continue through
  the low-dependency rustc crates toward the compiler binary.

- Measured pass (M9-06, fifth compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_hashes` now runs as a real dependency above the complete
  core/alloc/std stack and vendored `rustc-stable-hash`.  Its
  `FromStableHash::from` implementations destructure
  `SipHasher128Hash([low, high])` directly in a function parameter; HIR's
  narrow tuple-newtype signature form rejected that nested array pattern.
  A one-field tuple struct with an array parameter pattern now uses the
  existing synthetic ABI binding, leaving u-body to type and lower the nested
  leaves.  The generated 1,338,014-byte C unit has 1,782 reachable instances:
  1,509 real bodies, 245 shims, 28 residual stubs, and 17 vtables.  The probe
  exercises `Hash64`/`Hash128` construction, wrapping addition, XOR assignment,
  truncation, and accessors; it prints `rustc_hashes: 15 13` and exits 0 through
  real `lang_start` and cleanup.  Values stay within 64 bits because upper-word
  `u128` scalar storage remains a separate backend representation milestone.
  A standalone regression executes `Pair([left, right])` parameter binding.
  Gates: native suite, 143/143 standalone u-MIR fixtures, core-linked 4/4,
  and alloc-linked 2/2.  Next: continue through the low-dependency rustc crates
  toward the compiler binary.

- Measured pass (M9-06, sixth compiler-crate rung, probe-param88):
  `compiler/rustc_parse_format` now runs as a real dependency above the
  complete core/alloc/std stack, `rustc_lexer`, `rustc_literal_escaper`, and
  their vendored Unicode dependencies.  Concrete associated-type projections
  used as closure tuple inputs are normalized through a trait-indexed impl
  chain and cached, avoiding repeated whole-index scans while pending bodies
  converge.  The runtime slice also adds shared reborrows during impl lookup,
  closure calls through references, compiler-derived `Clone` fallback for
  concrete pointees, value-bearing `break`, and owned-box field projection.
  Focused regressions cover each of those boundaries.  The generated
  2,716,653-byte C unit has 2,908 reachable instances: 2,456 real bodies, 398
  shims, 54 residual stubs, and 19 vtables.  Its direct Rust root prints
  `rustc_parse_format: 10 1 true true true 0` and exits 0 after parsing a named
  argument with right alignment and numeric width.  The generated std
  `lang_start` path remains blocked independently in the known C-layout
  startup boundary (`pthread_attr_t` cleanup aborts with an invalid pointer),
  so this rung uses the same emitted graph with a native allocator/panic
  harness calling the Rust root directly.  Gates: native suite, 147/147
  standalone u-MIR fixtures, core-linked 4/4, and alloc-linked 2/2.  Next:
  continue through the low-dependency rustc crates toward the
  compiler binary, while retaining C-layout aggregates as the std-startup
  follow-up milestone.
